Result: 在测试的纯Clifford和近Clifford电路上,SymFT达到了最先进的采样性能。在单个CPU核心上,对于表面码电路,速度比Stim快2.51-2.56倍;对于魔态培养和蒸馏电路,速度比Clifft快1.86-3.51倍。特别是在测试的培养电路中,其采样吞吐量比之前的SOFT模拟器提高了两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: SymFT通过符号化框架分解和自适应坐标规划的创新组合,成功实现了Clifford主导量子电路的高效模拟,在多种容错协议电路类型上均展现出显著的性能优势,为量子纠错和容错量子计算的模拟研究提供了强大的工具,特别适用于包含少量非Clifford操作的大规模量子电路的快速采样分析。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant protocols often consist largely of stabilizer subcircuits, yet the non-Clifford operations required for universality make exact sampling costly. We present SymFT, a high-throughput simulator for Clifford-dominated circuits with Pauli rotations, stochastic Pauli noise, mid-circuit Pauli measurements, and measurement-record-controlled Pauli feedback. It combines two ideas. First, symbolic Clifford--Pauli frame factorization reduces branch-probability sampling to Pauli rotations and measurement projectors, with noise and feedback represented by symbolic signs. Since the residual Clifford and Pauli frames are unitary, they do not affect branch probabilities and need not be applied in every shot. Second, adaptive stabilizer-coordinate planning uses a shared stabilizer--destabilizer tableau to define the basis and stores only the active non-stabilizer degrees of freedom in a dynamically sized dense active-state vector. It resolves basis changes once and emits direct multi-coordinate sampling instructions, thereby avoiding per-shot tableau updates and localization-induced Clifford transformations of the dense vector. Across the tested pure-Clifford and near-Clifford circuits, SymFT achieves state-of-the-art sampling performance. On a single CPU core, it is $2.51\text{--}2.56\times$ faster than Stim for surface-code circuits and $1.86\text{--}3.51\times$ faster than Clifft for magic-state cultivation and distillation circuits. For the tested cultivation circuits, its sampling throughput also exceeds that of our previous simulator, SOFT, by more than two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [88] [Pauli Encodings & Unclonable Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28602)
*Pierre Botteron,Sébastien Designolle,Omar Fawzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子加密中的不可克隆比特问题,引入Pauli编码方案,证明了其单配性纠缠获胜概率的普遍下界为1/2+1/(2√K),建立了自然方法的局限性(XZ限制不安全、3/4上界障碍),并通过半定规划松弛和数值计算为强不可克隆安全性提供了理论和数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子加密能否防止对手在密钥公开后产生两个都能解密明文的系统(不可克隆比特问题)。现有研究缺乏对简单加密方案安全性边界的系统理解,需要建立普遍的理论框架来分析基于Pauli算符的量子加密方案的安全性极限,并探索实现不可克隆安全性的可行路径与固有障碍。

Method: 1) 定义Pauli编码:密文为K个Pauli串的归一化本征空间投影算符的单比特加密方案;2) 利用单配性纠缠(monogamy-of-entanglement)理论推导普遍下界公式;3) 针对结构化Pauli家族(如反对易、XZ限制等)进行专门分析;4) 采用半定规划(SDP)松弛的第三层NPA层级求解反对易情况的渐近上界;5) 针对有界局部维度对手证明强不可克隆不可区分安全性;6) 通过第一层NPA数值计算提供安全性的额外证据。

Result: 1) 证明了K个Pauli串编码的普遍下界为1/2+1/(2√K);2) 发现仅使用长度n的X和Z串的编码不安全;3) 建立了基于成对猜测边际分布论证的3/4普遍障碍;4) 对全反对易Pauli串情况,通过三阶SDP松弛获得约0.5556的渐近获胜概率上界;5) 证明了对有界局部维度对手的强不可克隆不可区分安全性;6) 对若干高效Pauli家族证明了强不可区分安全性;7) 一阶NPA计算为强不可克隆不可区分安全性提供了数值支持。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了Pauli编码的安全性理论框架,揭示了实现不可克隆加密的根本限制:普遍下界1/2+1/(2√K)表明需要足够多的Pauli串才能接近理想安全性,XZ限制和3/4障碍说明某些自然方法存在固有局限。反对易Pauli编码在对称性辅助下显示出更优的安全性能(上界约0.5556),且对受限对手具有可证明的强安全性。研究为量子不可克隆加密的可行性和优化方向提供了理论基础,但实现对任意对手的强安全性仍需进一步突破。

Abstract: The unclonable bit question asks whether quantum encryption can prevent an adversary from producing two systems that both reveal the plaintext once the key is disclosed. We introduce and study Pauli Encodings, a simple class of one-bit encryption schemes whose ciphertexts are normalized eigenspace projectors of Pauli strings. For every Pauli Encoding with K Pauli strings, we prove a universal lower bound $1/2+1/(2\sqrt{K})$ on the optimal monogamy-of-entanglement winning probability, together with sharper bounds for several structured families. We then establish two limitations of natural approaches to unclonable security. First, if the Pauli strings are restricted to strings of X and Z of length n, the encoding is not secure. Second, we identify a universal 3/4 obstruction showing that arguments based only on pairwise guessing marginals cannot establish unclonable-indistinguishable security. When the Pauli strings all pairwise anticommute, the protocol becomes the one studied in [Quantum 10, 2157 (2026)]. We exploit the symmetry of this protocol to solve the third level of the natural semidefinite programming relaxation obtaining an asymptotic upper bound of approximately 0.5556 on the winning probability. Finally, we prove strong unclonable-indistinguishable security against bounded-local-dimension adversaries and strong indistinguishability security for several efficient Pauli families. First-level NPA computations provide additional numerical evidence towards the strong unclonable-indistinguishable security.

</details>


### [89] [Logical computation with canonical lifted product codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28605)
*Han Zheng,Guo Zheng,Liang Jiang,Qian Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对高码率量子LDPC码的完整容错计算方案,通过与循环对称的典范提升积(LP)码协同设计,构建了典范逻辑基,实现了包括常数深度自同构门、模块化码手术、并行逻辑测量和魔态注入在内的完整逻辑指令集,显著降低了物理量子比特开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高码率量子LDPC码虽然能以低物理量子比特开销编码多个逻辑量子比特,但在这种密集编码上实现高效容错计算仍是重大挑战。现有的码手术和门隐形传态等通用技术难以在复杂高码率码上实现模块化、低开销且完全可认证的操作,因为它们未能充分利用码的内在结构。需要一种针对特定码族协同设计的方法来克服这些障碍。

Method: 研究者针对具有循环对称性的典范提升积(LP)码族,协同设计了码本身及其逻辑指令集。核心方法是构建"典范逻辑基",将共轭逻辑算符组织成直接继承自底层经典码的循环轨道的行和列结构(类似于超图积码的可处理结构)。基于这一典范基,开发了完整的逻辑操作工具包,包括:常数深度自同构和fold-transversal Clifford门、由常数个可复用种子手术小工具或紧凑典范提取器构建的模块化图码手术、高度并行的逻辑Pauli积测量,以及并行魔态注入。

Result: 对于[[1122,148,≤20]]码,仅需两个种子手术小工具;对于[[4350,1224,≤20]]码,仅需四个种子手术小工具。任意高权重逻辑测量可使用小于数据码块一半大小的完整提取器实现。这些结果展示了该方法在超高码率量子架构上的实用性和高效性,显著降低了资源开销。

Conclusion: 本研究通过为循环对称的典范提升积码构建典范逻辑基,成功解决了高码率量子LDPC码上容错计算的模块化、低开销和可认证性难题。所提出的完整逻辑指令集(包括常数深度门操作、模块化码手术和并行测量)推进了超高码率量子架构上容错量子计算的前沿,为实现高效、可扩展的量子计算提供了重要技术路径。

Abstract: High-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes encode many logical qubits with low physical-qubit overhead, but realizing efficient fault-tolerant computation on such dense encodings remains a major challenge. Generic, code-agnostic techniques such as code surgery and gate teleportation apply broadly, but are difficult to make modular, low-overhead, and fully certifiable on complex high-rate codes whose structure is left unexploited. Here we overcome these obstacles by co-designing the code together with its logical instruction set for a broad family of \emph{canonical} lifted-product (LP) codes with cyclic symmetry. We show that these codes admit a \emph{canonical logical basis}, in which conjugate logical operators are organized into rows and columns of cyclic orbits inherited directly from the underlying classical codes, analogous to the structure that makes hypergraph-product codes so tractable. This canonical basis unlocks a complete logical instruction set, including constant-depth automorphism and fold-transversal Clifford gates, modular graph code surgeries built from a constant number of reusable seed surgery gadgets or a compact canonical extractor, highly parallel logical Pauli-product measurements, and parallel magic-state injection. For example, a $[[1122,148,\leq\!20]]$ (resp. $[[4350,1224,\leq\!20]]$) LP code requires only two (resp. four) seed surgery gadgets, while arbitrary high-weight logical measurements can be implemented using a full extractor smaller than half of the data code block. These results advance the frontier of fault-tolerant quantum computation on ultra-high-rate quantum architectures.

</details>


### [90] [Learning Arbitrary Lindbladians from Time Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28610)
*Zhili Chen,Zhan Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效算法,用于从物理时间演化中学习未知的马尔可夫开放系统生成器(Lindbladian),该算法在实验次数和总演化时间上接近最优,仅差对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习量子开放系统的Lindbladian生成器面临指数级大的Pauli项族挑战。现有方法缺乏在最小假设下高效学习任意Lindbladian的能力,需要开发一种能够识别支撑集并精确估计哈密顿量和耗散系数的多项式时间算法。

Method: 该算法包含两个非自适应、无辅助比特、无控制的阶段:1) 支撑集学习阶段:使用乘积Pauli本征态制备和单量子比特Pauli测量,通过$\widetilde O(Λ^2/η^2)$次实验输出包含所有大于等于$η$的哈密顿量和耗散坐标的候选支撑集;2) 系数学习阶段:使用随机稳定子态制备和随机Clifford基测量,通过$\widetilde O(Λ^2\log M/ε^{2})$次实验将大小为$M$的候选支撑集中所有系数估计到误差$ε$。

Result: 对于动力学强度最大为$Λ$的Lindbladian,算法使用$\widetilde O(Λ^2/ε^2)$次实验和$\widetilde O(Λ/ε^2)$总演化时间,以及多项式经典运行时间,将每个系数估计到误差$ε$。组合两个阶段可在多项式时间内识别并估计任意Lindbladian的每个系数。实验次数和总演化时间的标度与下界仅差对数因子。

Conclusion: 该算法实现了学习任意Lindbladian的近最优性能,在实验复杂度和时间复杂度上达到了理论下界(相差对数因子)。算法的非自适应、无辅助、无控制特性使其具有实际可行性,为量子开放系统的表征提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning an unknown Markovian open-system generator from access to its physical time evolution. This generator, called a Lindbladian, contains Hamiltonian and dissipative coefficients indexed by an exponentially large family of possible Pauli terms. We propose an efficient algorithm that learns arbitrary Lindbladians from time evolution under minimal assumptions. For a Lindbladian of dynamical strength at most $Λ$, the algorithm estimates every coefficient to error $ε$ using $\widetilde O(Λ^2/ε^2)$ experiments and $\widetilde O(Λ/ε^2)$ total evolution time, together with polynomial classical running time. The algorithm consists of two nonadaptive, ancilla-free, and control-free stages: 1. The support-learning stage outputs a candidate support of size $\mathrm{poly}(Λ/η)$ that contains every Hamiltonian and dissipative coordinate of magnitude at least $η$, using $\widetilde O(Λ^2/η^2)$ experiments with preparations of product Pauli eigenstates and single-qubit Pauli measurements. 2.The coefficient-learning stage estimates all coefficients in any candidate support of size $M$ to error $ε$, using $\widetilde O(Λ^2\log M/ε^{2})$ experiments with preparations of random stabilizer states and measurements in random Clifford bases. Composing the two stages identifies and estimates every coefficient of an arbitrary Lindbladian in polynomial time. The experiment-count and total-evolution-time scalings match the lower bounds up to logarithmic factors, so the algorithm is nearly optimal for learning arbitrary Lindbladians.

</details>


### [91] [On Optimal Measurement-State Preparation via Geometric Transport of the Squeezing Ellipse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28620)
*Vsevolod Salakhutdinov,Nikolay Kalinin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何框架，用于将初始未对齐的压缩态转换为SU(2)对称系统中的最优测量态，揭示了压缩椭圆旋转与路径几何及几何相位之间的联系，并以偏振压缩光为例给出了实现方案。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计量学中，利用压缩态进行精密测量需要制备最优测量态。然而，初始压缩态往往与测量所需的最优方向不匹配。因此，需要一种系统性的方法将未对齐的压缩态转换为最优测量态，同时理解这种转换过程中压缩椭圆方向演化的几何性质。

Method: 建立了一个几何框架来处理SU(2)对称系统中压缩态的转换问题。在该框架中，将压缩椭圆的方向作为额外的几何自由度，当平均态沿单位球面上的受控轨迹演化时，压缩椭圆也随之旋转。通过分析轨迹的几何特性，证明了椭圆旋转由路径几何决定，特别是与轨迹所围立体角相关，从而建立了与几何相位的联系。以偏振压缩光为例，提出使用连续变化的双折射元件来实现该框架。

Result: 证明了在相关变换类别中，压缩椭圆的旋转取决于态轨迹在单位球面上所围成的立体角，这与几何相位具有直接关联。该几何框架具有普适性，可应用于不同的物理平台。针对偏振压缩光的具体案例，给出了基于连续变化双折射元件的可行实现方案。

Conclusion: 本文建立的几何框架为量子计量学中制备最优测量态提供了系统性的理论方法，揭示了压缩态方向演化的深层几何结构及其与几何相位的本质联系。该框架具有平台无关性，可广泛应用于不同量子系统，为实际量子精密测量提供了理论指导和实验实现途径。

Abstract: Preparation of optimal measurement states is a key requirement in quantum metrology utilizing squeezed states. We discuss a geometric framework that transforms an initially misaligned squeezed input into a measurement-optimal state in SU(2)-symmetric systems. Within this framework, the orientation of the squeezing ellipse constitutes an additional geometric degree of freedom and evolves as the mean state follows a controlled trajectory on the unit sphere. The resulting rotation of the ellipse is determined by the geometry of the path and, for the relevant class of transformations, depends on the solid angle enclosed by the trajectory, establishing a connection with the geometric phase. The discussed framework is applicable to different physical platforms. As a particular example, we consider polarization-squeezed light and outline a possible implementation using a continuously varying birefringent element.

</details>


### [92] [Lifting Lifted Product Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28621)
*Yuta Hirasaki,Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于群扩展和图提升的系统化lifted product (LP)码构造方法,通过保持Tanner图局部结构的同时增加码规模,实现了更优的码参数、逻辑算子传递和容错逻辑操作,并探索了代数定义的qLDPC码的热力学族构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的lifted product码虽然具有良好的码参数,但缺乏系统化的构造方法来优化码族参数、传递逻辑算子和容错操作gadget,并且代数定义的qLDPC码在没有欧几里得格子基础时难以定义热力学族。需要一种能够在保持局部结构的同时扩展码规模,并系统关联码族内部各种性质的构造框架。

Method: 提出基于群扩展(group extensions)和图提升(graph lifts)的系统化LP码构造方法。通过有限提升增加码规模的同时保持Tanner图的局部结构,利用链映射(chain maps)和上链映射(cochain maps)关联码族内的码参数、逻辑算子和容错逻辑操作gadgets。针对lifted clustered cyclic码开发并行积手术(parallel product surgery)。将提升作为系统化的第一步来定义代数qLDPC码的热力学族,通过分析相干信息的有限尺寸交叉行为来识别候选族。

Result: 获得了码参数优于先前报道的LP码;成功通过链映射在选定的有限提升间传递码手术gadgets,在多个案例中降低了空间开销;开发的并行积手术适用于lifted clustered cyclic码;对多个基础码和选定提升,相干信息展现出有限尺寸交叉现象,表明提升方法可作为定义热力学族的有效起点,但需要额外条件来确定唯一的码族。

Conclusion: 基于群扩展和图提升的系统化构造为LP码族提供了统一的理论框架,不仅能够产生参数更优的量子纠错码,还能够系统地传递和优化容错操作gadgets。该方法为没有欧几里得格子结构的代数qLDPC码定义热力学族提供了可行路径,尽管确定唯一热力学族仍需进一步的理论条件。这一构造方法为量子纠错码的系统化设计和优化开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Lifted product (LP) codes form an important class of quantum error correcting codes with favorable code parameters. We introduce a systematic construction of LP code families based on group extensions and graph lifts. The construction increases the code size while preserving the local structure of the Tanner graph, and relates code parameters, logical operators, and fault-tolerant logical-operation gadgets within the families through chain and cochain maps.
  As a first application, we obtain LP codes with better code parameters than previously reported ones. We then demonstrate that code-surgery gadgets can be transferred across the selected finite lifts through chain maps and, in several cases, implemented with lower space overhead. We also develop parallel product surgery for lifted clustered cyclic codes. Finally, we propose lifting as a systematic first step toward defining thermodynamic families for algebraically defined qLDPC codes without an underlying Euclidean lattice. For several base codes and selected lifts, coherent information exhibits finite-size crossings, while our results also indicate that additional conditions are needed to determine a unique family.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文通过原始构造方法系统地研究了无限自旋塔振幅，解析地推导出的允许区域边界在无累积点情况下是最大可能边界，该边界略小于但接近正定性界方法的结果。研究揭示了引力子极点对紫外谱的幺正性约束，为理解散射振幅的物理约束提供了新的视角，表明"包含法"与"排除法"两种方法在确定物理允许区域方面具有互补性。

Abstract: We study the general form of meromorphic amplitudes that are compatible with unitarity, analyticity, crossing symmetry, polynomial boundedness and the hidden-zero and corresponding splitting conditions. These amplitudes are infinite-spin-tower (IST) amplitudes and are characterized by the distribution of poles. With infinitely many evenly separated poles, the IST amplitudes reduce to Veneziano amplitudes. We construct such unitary amplitudes in a primal way (rule in) and find the bounds analytically. The allowed region we derive is smaller than, but close to, the region allowed by the positivity bounds (rule out). We argue that, in the absence of an accumulation point in the energy levels, the analytic boundary we derive is the largest possible boundary in the primal construction of meromorphic amplitudes. This type of IST amplitude can also be extended to the fully crossing-symmetric case related to the Virasoro-Shapiro amplitude. We found from the IST amplitudes that graviton pole imposes unitarity constraints on UV spectrum.

Abstract: We point out that observations of cosmic birefringence may provide direct experimental tests of Swampland conjectures. Ultralight scalar field birefringence mechanisms are constrained by Weak Gravity Conjecture, limiting their parameter space. Conversely, confirming that CMB birefringence is caused by ultralight scalars could spell problems for the Swampland framework. Resolving these difficulties without a revision of Swampland ideology points toward thin axionic domain walls, which can explain birefringence without propagating ultralight degrees of freedom. Importantly these conflicting scenarios for cosmic birefringence could be experimentally distinguished by a search for, or exclusion of, birefringence anisotropies, that could be measured by POLARBEAR, Simons Observatory and LiteBIRD. We also note that cosmic birefringence observables emerge via a Sakharov-like asymmetry, where linear polarization is first generated cosmologically and subsequently twisted by a parity-violating dynamics after last scattering.

</details>

Abstract: We apply the framework of Soft Collinear Effective Theory (SCET) to the theory of the geometric scalar field. The resulting ``geoSCET'' manifests an emergent geometry in the soft sector, mirroring the emergent soft gauge invariance of QCD SCET. We use geoSCET to derive the geometric soft theorems as straightforward consequences of effective-field-theory power counting, including extensions to multiple soft emissions and loop corrections. We demonstrate that theories without a potential satisfy universal geometric soft theorems for any number of soft emissions to all orders in perturbation theory. We also show that we can turn on a potential at the soft scale without spoiling the factorization of the soft physics. This work demonstrates the underlying field-space diffeomorphism origin of the geometric soft theorem.

Abstract: We consider three families of semiclassical string solutions on AdS$_5\times S^5/\mathbb{Z}_L$ orbifold backgrounds which correspond to `twisted' single-trace operators in the SU$(2)$, SU$(3)$ and SL$(2)$ sectors, respectively, of the dual 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ quiver gauge theory. The leading quantum correction to the classical energy of each solution is computed, and is matched, despite the lack of maximal supersymmetry, to the corresponding results from finite-size corrections to the twisted Bethe ansatz in the continuum $J\to\infty$ limit and the associated Landau-Lifshitz low-energy effective theory. The $\mathbb{Z}_L$ quotient allows the string to have fractional windings $μ$ along orbifolded directions, resulting in novel stable subsectors of semiclassical states that are absent in the analogous semiclassical string solutions on AdS$_5\times S^5$. The energies of these stable states connect smoothly to those of states in relevant BPS sectors as $μ\to0$. Motivated by recent localisation results on the dual gauge theory, we comment on the `long quiver' regime $L\gg\sqrtλ\gg 1$ from a semiclassical perspective, concluding that it reorganises the standard fixed-background semiclassical expansion.

Abstract: Within the semi-classical regime of AdS/CFT correspondence, we consider the limit where the bulk dynamical field is linearized metric perturbations satisfying linearized Einstein equations over background pure AdS spacetime. AdS/CFT correspondence gives us a holographic map, which is an isometric embedding map of the GNS Hilbert space of linearized gravity in the bulk (w.r.t. the AdS-invariant vacuum) to the GNS Hilbert space of CFT in the boundary (w.r.t. the Minkowski-invariant vacuum). We assume that the map takes AdS-vacuum in the bulk to CFT-vacuum in the boundary and that it allows AdS-Rindler wedge reconstruction. Then using this map, we show that for a given ball-shaped region in the boundary $A$, the relative entropy of a bulk state w.r.t. the AdS vacuum in the algebra of causal wedge associated to $A$ matches with the relative entropy of the dual CFT state w.r.t. the CFT vacuum in the algebra of CFT observables in $A$ in the code subspace, which is known as Jafferis-Lewkowycz-Maldacena-Suh (JLMS) condition. Furthermore, for localized semi-classical coherent excitations in the causal wedge associated to $A$ which corresponds to perturbed bulk geometry, we show rigorously using modular crossed product construction that the state-dependent part of entropy of the dual CFT state in the dressed Type-II algebra associated to $A$ satisfies vacuum subtracted Hubeney-Rangamani-Takayanagi (HRT) formula.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管非线性sigma模型是描述SU(N)戈德斯通玻色子散射的重要有效场论,但其传统形式包含无穷多的二阶导数相互作用塔,使得费曼图计算复杂。研究者希望找到一种更简洁的表述形式,以揭示NLSM隐藏的简单性结构,并提高多粒子振幅计算的效率。

Method: 引入包含两个携带相反"极性"的标量场ψ⁺和ψ⁻的局部标量拉格朗日量,其相互作用部分仅由单个多项式四次二阶导数算符构成。通过这种四次二阶导数形式化,研究者在所有圈层的广义割上证明该理论能够再现平面NLSM振幅,并使Adler零点和领头双软因子在所有圈层都显而易见。

Result: 成功证明了新提出的四次二阶导数拉格朗日量在所有圈层再现平面NLSM振幅。新形式的费曼规则仅涉及单个四次相互作用顶点,使得Adler零点和领头双软因子在所有圈层的广义割上变得显然,显著提高了高重数计算的效率。

Conclusion: 通过引入双标量场的四次二阶导数形式,揭示了非线性sigma模型此前隐藏的简单性结构。这种新表述将无穷多相互作用塔简化为单个四次顶点,不仅在理论上更加优雅,而且在实际计算中更加高效。研究还提出了用有限局部相互作用塔类似描述NLSM+φ³理论的可能性,为进一步研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We revisit the well-known nonlinear sigma model (NLSM), an effective field theory describing the scattering of $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ Goldstone bosons and characterized by an infinite tower of two-derivative interactions. We introduce a local scalar Lagrangian involving two scalar fields $ψ^\pm$ carrying opposite "polarities", whose interacting part consists of a single polynomial quartic two-derivative operator, and prove that it reproduces planar NLSM amplitudes at all loop orders. This quartic two-derivative formulation reveals a previously hidden simplicity of the NLSM: its Feynman rules involve only a single quartic interaction vertex, making the Adler zero and the leading double-soft factor manifest at all loop orders on generalized cuts and significantly improving the efficiency of high-multiplicity computations. We also suggest a possible analogous description of the NLSM +$ φ^3 $ theory in terms of a finite tower of local interactions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 研究发现了丰富多样的动力学可能性,包括场空间中的转动和原初扰动的增强。在大部分参数空间中,特别是当轴子具有远小于普朗克尺度的衰变常数(f≪M_Pl)时,对曲率扰动功率谱的影响可以忽略,表明模型具有整体稳健性。但当轴子具有较大但仍为亚普朗克的衰变常数(f≳O(0.1)M_Pl)且非微扰势具有指数小的前因子时,可以增强扰动的增长,更容易实现种子原初黑洞所需的放大效应。

Abstract: We study the impact of light spectator axions on the seeding of primordial black holes (PBHs) during inflation in string theory. Primordial black holes exhibit unique and novel phenomenology, and may constitute the observed dark matter. Cosmic inflation provides a mechanism for producing them, but such inflation models typically feature Planckian field excursions, necessitating an ultraviolet completion into quantum gravity. String theory provides a natural framework for doing so, and indeed Fibre Inflation has been shown to produce PBHs while satisfying constraints from cosmic microwave background data. In this work we study the dynamics of axions during Fibre Inflation, and find a diverse and rich set of possibilities, including turns in field space and enhancement of primordial perturbations. We find that across most of parameter space, notably an axion with a far sub-Planckian decay constant $f\ll M_{\rm Pl}$, there is a negligible impact on the power spectrum of curvature perturbations, indicating an overall robustness of the model. On the other hand, an axion with a larger but still sub-Planckian decay constant, $f\gtrsim {\cal O}(0.1) M_{\rm Pl}$, and an exponentially small prefactor of its non-perturbative potential, can enhance the growth of perturbations, making it easier to achieve the amplification needed to seed PBHs, effectively realizing axion-assisted PBHs in string theory.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用Faddeev-Jackiw辛约束方法对每个理论进行分析:首先确定相空间及其辛结构;识别余迷向约束子流形;构造哈密顿量;分析约束的可约性性质;最后结合Dirac猜想,通过直接计算重构相应的拉格朗日规范变换及其可约性结构。该方法应用于Freedman-Townsend模型、Yang-Mills理论、非阿贝尔BF模型以及非线性BF型理论。

Result: 对所研究的每个非阿贝尔规范理论,成功确定了:完整的相空间及辛结构、余迷向约束子流形的具体形式、相应的哈密顿量表达式、约束的可约性性质,以及通过直接计算重构的拉格朗日规范变换和规范对称性的可约性结构。这些结果展示了不同非阿贝尔理论之间在约束结构上的共性和差异。

Conclusion: Faddeev-Jackiw方法为分析非阿贝尔规范理论提供了一个统一、高效且直接的计算框架。该方法能够系统地推导出这些理论的主要哈密顿和拉格朗日规范结构,避免了传统Dirac方法中的一些繁琐步骤。本文的分析证明了该方法在处理复杂非阿贝尔规范理论时的有效性和优越性,为进一步研究更广泛的规范理论及其量子化提供了有力工具。

Abstract: This paper illustrates the straightforward application of the Faddeev--Jackiw approach to several non-Abelian gauge theories, namely, the Freedman--Townsend, Yang--Mills, and non-Abelian BF models, as well as a nonlinear theory of BF type. For each theory, the procedure determines the phase space and its symplectic structure, the coisotropic constraint submanifold, the Hamiltonian, and the reducibility properties of the constraints. By combining these results with the Dirac conjecture, one reconstructs through direct computation the corresponding Lagrangian gauge transformations and their reducibility structure. The analysis provides a unified and efficient derivation of the principal Hamiltonian and Lagrangian gauge structures of these non-Abelian theories.

</details>


### [101] [$w_{1+\infty}$ as the Frame Algebra of Kerr Soft Dressing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27469)

Result: 证明了Veneziano-Vilkovisky超平移产生一个由指数化软展开选择的塔结构，该对象在三点处生成Kerr多极矩。由于指数因子分解为偶部分和奇部分，塔结构呈现交替宇称特性。对于自旋对齐情况，成功用双曲积分解出该塔结构的所有阶次。经过手征投影后，其复合律精确对应于经典$w_{1+\infty}$括号。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了Veneziano-Vilkovisky超平移的深层数学结构：它不仅是连接不同坐标系的几何变换，更编码了Kerr黑洞多极矩的生成机制。通过指数化软展开和手征投影，该超平移的代数结构对应于经典$w_{1+\infty}$无穷维代数，为该抽象代数赋予了明确的物理解释——即描述引力散射中的软模式和大规范变换的代数结构。这为理解量子引力的对称性结构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The Veneziano--Vilkovisky supertranslation is the residual large diffeomorphism relating the canonical Bondi frame to the intrinsic frame of the scattering bodies. We show it leads a tower selected by the exponentiating soft expansion, the object generating the Kerr multipoles at three points. Since \(e^{ηωa\cdot q}\) splits into even and odd parts, the tower alternates parity, and for aligned spin we solve it to all orders in hyperbolic integrals. After a chiral projection its composition law is the classical $w_{1+\infty}$ bracket: the physical content we assign to that algebra.

</details>

*Gabriel Menezes*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了与Kerr选定软装扮相关的电荷生成的内禀/正则坐标系字典，通过将软核与普适软贡献的Kerr选定指数化投影相匹配，求解了高自旋问题的逆问题，并揭示了w_{1+∞}类结构在Kerr黑洞散射中的物理作用——它驱动内禀/正则字典的变化，其可观测印记始于位移记忆和自旋记忆，随后是更高阶的电磁记忆矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑洞散射和引力波物理中，需要理解软引力子定理与黑洞多极矩结构之间的深层联系。特别是需要建立Kerr黑洞的软装扮与BMS对称性框架之间的精确对应关系，并阐明w_{1+∞}代数结构在Kerr黑洞散射中的物理意义。现有理论缺乏对高自旋情况下电荷生成的坐标系字典的系统构造，以及对不同宇称分量如何通过Kerr选择机制确定的完整理解。

Method: 从VV超平移出发，将高自旋问题表述为零无穷远处的逆问题：通过宇称适配的最大纵向标量χ^{(s)}_t构建软核K^{(s,0)}_{AB}[t],并将其与普适软贡献的Kerr选定指数化投影相匹配，从而确定生成元t^{A_1...A_s}的一个宇称分量。利用螺旋度共轭关系确定具体分量:指数化源满足关系式,使得偶数层固定源的电投影,奇数层固定磁投影,与Kerr质量和电流矩的交替模式匹配。对于对齐自旋情况,以闭合形式求解整个塔结构。通过T*S^2上的多项式泊松代数及其局部w_{1+∞}型约化作用于这些坐标系变换生成元,分析其代数结构和物理效应。

Result: 该方案在领头阶再现了VV超平移,在次领头阶固定了光滑广义BMS矢量的旋度而非散度。证明了相同的指数化软因子是Guevara-Ochirov-Vines旋转三点算符的经典极限,并生成Kerr多极塔。建立了相应的硬流荷,并展示其外态作用如何给出软定理的Ward表示。T*S^2上的多项式泊松代数通过局部w_{1+∞}型约化作用于坐标系变换生成元,但不能仅在Kerr选定数据上闭合。确定了w_{1+∞}类结构的物理作用:它驱动内禀/正则字典的移动,其可观测印记从s=0的位移记忆和s=1的自旋记忆开始,随后是更高阶的电磁记忆矩。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了Kerr选定软装扮的电荷生成内禀/正则坐标系字典,解决了零无穷远处的高自旋逆问题。揭示了w_{1+∞}类代数结构在Kerr黑洞散射中的核心物理意义:它不是一个封闭的对称性,而是驱动内禀与正则坐标系之间字典转换的动力学机制。这一结构的可观测效应体现为从低阶到高阶的记忆效应序列(位移记忆、自旋记忆及更高电磁记忆矩),为理解黑洞散射中的软定理、BMS对称性和引力记忆效应提供了统一的理论框架,并建立了软引力子物理与Kerr黑洞多极结构之间的精确对应关系。

Abstract: We construct the charge-generated intrinsic/canonical frame dictionary associated with the Kerr-selected soft dressing. Starting from the VV supertranslation, we formulate the higher-spin problem as an inverse problem at null infinity: the soft kernel ${\mathcal K}^{(s,0)}_{AB}[t]$, built from the parity-adapted maximally longitudinal scalar $χ^{(s)}_t$, is matched to the Kerr-selected exponentiating projection of the universal soft contribution, thereby determining one parity component of the generator $t^{A_1\cdots A_s}$. Helicity conjugation fixes which one: the exponentiating source obeys $\overline{S^{(s)}_{+,{\rm exp}}}=(-1)^sS^{(s)}_{-,{\rm exp}}$, so the tower fixes the electric projection of the source at even levels and the magnetic projection at odd ones, matching the alternation of the Kerr mass and current moments; for aligned spin the projection is exhaustive and we solve the tower in closed form. The prescription reproduces the VV supertranslation at leading order and fixes the curl, not the divergence, of a smooth generalized-BMS vector at subleading order. The reason for the matching is physical: the same exponentiating soft factor is the classical limit of the Guevara--Ochirov--Vines spinning three-point operator and generates the Kerr multipole tower. We explain the corresponding hard flux charges and show how their external-state action gives the Ward representation of the soft theorem. The polynomial Poisson algebra on $T^\ast S^2$, with local $w_{1+\infty}$-type reductions, then acts on these frame-changing generators; it does not close on the Kerr-selected data alone. This gives the physical role of the $w_{1+\infty}$-like structure in Kerr black-hole scattering: it moves the intrinsic/canonical dictionary. Its observable imprint begins with displacement memory at $s=0$ and spin memory at $s=1$, followed by higher electric and magnetic memory moments.

</details>

*Diptarka Das,Sumit R. Das,Arnab Kundu,Krishnendu Sengupta*

Method: 建立了d维空间中非相对论共形场论的Floquet动力学形式体系。通过计算驱动过程中多体基态上共形生成元的期望值以及态保真度来表征系统行为。利用频闪观测分析不同相的动力学特征。从全息对偶角度研究bulk时空几何与边界动力学相的对应关系,分析Killing视界和遍历层等几何结构。

Result: 发现了双曲相和椭圆相两个动力学相,由抛物线过渡面分隔。双曲相中共形生成元期望值和保真度呈指数行为;椭圆相中表现为振荡行为;过渡面上呈现幂律行为。该分析具有完全普适性,可直接应用于幺正极限附近的束缚费米子和共振任意子等多个系统。从全息角度发现双曲相对应bulk中的类时静止极限面(如遍历层),抛物相对应极端Killing视界。

Conclusion: 成功建立了非相对论共形不变系统Floquet动力学的普适理论框架,揭示了双曲相、椭圆相及其抛物过渡的普适动力学特征。该理论可应用于幺正极限附近的束缚费米子等实验可实现系统,为观测这些动力学相提供了明确的实验特征。全息分析建立了边界动力学相与bulk几何结构的深刻对应,为理解非相对论共形场论的量子动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: We develop a formalism for studying Floquet dynamics for systems with {\it non-relativistic} conformal invariance in $d$ spatial dimensions. Our analysis indicates the existence of two dynamical phases, hyperbolic and elliptic, separated by a parabolic transition surface. We demonstrate this by studying the expectation value of a conformal generator in the many body ground state during the drive, as well as the fidelity of this state. Stroboscopically, they behave exponentially in the hyperbolic phase, show oscillatory behavior in the elliptic phase, and exhibit power-law on the transition surface. Our analysis is completely universal and can be directly applied to several systems including trapped fermions near unitarity and resonant anyons. The former can provide experimental signatures of these dynamical phases. We also comment on a holographic perspective of such driven non-relativistic CFTs and demonstrate that the hyperbolic phase is associated with a timelike stationary-limit surface, such as an ergosphere, in the bulk, while the parabolic phase corresponds to an extremal Killing horizon.

</details>


### [104] [Vacuum fluctuations in Rainbow space-time: Study of Casimir effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27722)
*Bhagya. R,Suman Kumar Panja*

TL;DR: 本文研究了彩虹时空中的卡西米尔效应,通过求解标量场的格林函数获得了修正的卡西米尔能量和力的表达式,发现不同彩虹函数选择会导致卡西米尔力的不同变化行为,并通过实验数据给出彩虹参数的约束上限约为10^-24量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卡西米尔效应研究基于标准闵可夫斯基时空,而彩虹时空作为量子引力的一种唯象模型,可能在高能标下修正时空结构。研究彩虹时空中的卡西米尔效应有助于理解量子引力效应对真空能量的影响,并为检验彩虹引力理论提供可观测的物理约束。

Method: 首先从彩虹时空中的标量场拉格朗日量出发,利用δ函数势引入平行板边界条件,推导出相应的能量-动量张量。通过将能量-动量张量的真空期望值表示为作用于格林函数的二次算符形式,求解彩虹时空中标量场的欧拉-拉格朗日方程得到格林函数解。将格林函数代入能量-动量张量的真空期望值表达式,从而获得彩虹时空中修正的卡西米尔能量和卡西米尔力的解析表达式。

Result: 研究发现对于不同的彩虹函数选择,卡西米尔能量和力呈现不同的行为特征:两种彩虹函数选择导致卡西米尔能量和力的绝对值相对于标准结果出现增大或减小的修正;而对于一种特定的彩虹函数选择,结果与标准闵可夫斯基时空中的结果保持一致。通过将理论结果与卡西米尔力的实验测量值进行比较,获得了彩虹参数相关项的约束上限为10^-24量级。

Conclusion: 本研究成功建立了彩虹时空框架下卡西米尔效应的理论计算方法,揭示了彩虹函数的选择对卡西米尔能量和力修正的关键影响。通过实验数据对比,给出了彩虹参数的严格约束(~10^-24),表明在当前可观测尺度下彩虹引力效应极其微弱,为彩虹引力理论的实验检验提供了重要的参考依据,也为进一步探索量子引力效应在宏观量子现象中的表现开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the Casimir effect in rainbow space-time, focusing on leading-order corrections to the Casimir energy and force. Starting with the scalar field Lagrangian in rainbow space-time, with parallel plates introduced through delta-function potentials, we find the corresponding energy-momentum tensor. We obtain the vacuum expectation value of this energy-momentum tensor by expressing it as a quadratic operator acting on the Green's function. By solving the Euler-Lagrange equation of a scalar field in rainbow space-time, we obtain the Green's function solutions. Employing these Green's function solutions in the vacuum expectation value of the energy-momentum tensor, we obtain the modified Casimir energy and Casimir force expressions in rainbow space-time. We study the variation of the deformed Casimir force and energy with the distance between the plates for different choices of rainbow functions. Our results show that for two choices of rainbow functions, the absolute value of the Casimir energy and force is decreasing or increasing, whereas for one specific choice of rainbow functions, it remains the same as the standard result in Minkowski space-time. Comparing our result with experimentally measured value of Casimir force, we obtain the bound on the rainbow parameter dependent terms to be of the order of 10^-24.

</details>


### [105] [A comparison of two constructions for dynamical corrections to Wald entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27732)
*Sayantani Bhattacharyya,Parthajit Biswas,Nilay Kundu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析比较了两种将Wald熵从稳态黑洞推广到非稳态情况的构造方法：基于近视界几何boost对称性的$S_\text{Wall}$和基于Wald-Iyer Noether荷协变相空间分析的$S_\text{dyn}$，阐明了二者的根本差异，并开发了从$S_\text{dyn}$构造$S_\text{Wall}$的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高阶导数引力理论中，需要将Wald熵从稳态黑洞推广到非稳态情况。已有两种不同方法学的构造（$S_\text{Wall}$和$S_\text{dyn}$），但它们之间的根本差异、相互关系以及转换方法尚不清楚，需要系统性的理论分析和比较研究。

Method: 通过对比分析两种构造的技术结构来阐明其基础方法的根本差异；研究从$S_\text{Wall}$构造$S_\text{dyn}$的直接方法和反向转换的复杂性；开发了一种算法，用于在满足特定技术条件下从已知$S_\text{dyn}$表达式获得通用微分同胚不变引力理论中$S_\text{Wall}$的局域表达式；在Riemann平方高阶导数引力理论中进行显式验证。

Result: 证明了从$S_\text{Wall}$构造$S_\text{dyn}$是直接的，但反向构造更加微妙复杂；成功开发了适用于通用微分同胚不变引力理论的算法，可从$S_\text{dyn}$获得$S_\text{Wall}$的局域表达式；在Riemann平方高阶导数引力理论的具体案例中通过显式计算验证了分析结果的正确性；澄清了两种构造方法的本质区别：$S_\text{Wall}$不需要线性化即可构造且自然包含高阶项，而$S_\text{dyn}$仅定义于稳态黑洞解的线性化涨落。

Conclusion: 本研究系统阐明了两种黑洞熵构造方法（$S_\text{Wall}$和$S_\text{dyn}$）的根本差异和内在联系。虽然$S_\text{Wall}$可以直接导出$S_\text{dyn}$，但反向构造需要特定条件和专门算法。研究为高阶导数引力理论中非稳态黑洞熵的计算提供了系统的理论框架和实用工具，并通过具体案例验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we analyze the differences and similarities between two recent constructions, which are distinct in their methodologies for extending the Wald entropy of stationary black holes to non-stationary situations in general higher-derivative gravity. One of them, denoted by $S_\text{Wall}$, is constructed by exploiting the boost symmetry of the near-horizon geometry, whereas the other, denoted by $S_\text{dyn}$, is obtained from a covariant phase-space analysis based on the Wald-Iyer Noether charge formalism. While $S_\text{dyn}$ is, by construction, defined only for linearized fluctuations around a stationary black hole solution, $S_\text{Wall}$ does not require such a linearization for its construction. Although the linearization is necessary to interpret $S_\text{Wall}$ as a well-defined notion of entropy, the construction itself naturally contains terms that are higher order in the dynamical fluctuations. By comparing the technical structures underlying the two constructions, we clarify the fundamental differences between the methods on which they are based. We demonstrate that while the construction of $S_\text{dyn}$ given the $S_\text{Wall}$ is straightforward, the converse is more subtle. We develop an algorithm to obtain a local expression for $S_\text{Wall}$ from a known expression for $S_\text{dyn}$ in a generic diffeomorphism-invariant theory of gravity, provided certain technical conditions are satisfied. We justify our analytical findings with explicit demonstrations in a particular case: the Riemann-squared example of the higher-derivative theory of gravity.

</details>


### [106] [Non-conformal obstructions to bubble expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27874)
*David Mateos,Mikel Sanchez-Garitaonandia,Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一阶相变中膨胀气泡的流体动力学行为,特别关注非共形热力学效应。研究发现了新的流体动力学障碍机制和"激波爆轰"解,这些效应显著限制了气泡壁速度的允许范围,并对引力波信号产生重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的相变气泡膨胀研究主要基于共形热力学或简单的袋模型描述,但真实物理系统(如电弱相变和QCD相变)具有非共形特性。现有模型可能无法准确描述气泡动力学和预测引力波信号,因此需要系统研究非共形热力学对一阶相变气泡膨胀的影响,以提高相变引力波谱建模的准确性。

Method: 研究采用了以下方法:(1)分析了从袋模型到QCD类理论的广泛状态方程类别;(2)以纯SU(3)杨-米尔斯理论作为具体基准案例进行计算;(3)系统求解不同参数下的气泡流体动力学解;(4)识别和分类新的解类型和流体动力学障碍;(5)开发并公开了构建气泡解的计算代码;(6)使用全息方法展示如何通过改变物质场对几何的反作用来实现袋模型与QCD类极限之间的插值。

Result: 研究获得了以下主要结果:(1)发现了一组新的流体动力学障碍,这些障碍出现在气泡壁和流体流动路径上,可以部分或完全排除原本允许的解;(2)识别出一类新解——"激波爆轰"解,由普通爆轰加上稀疏波中插入的额外激波组成;(3)发现允许的气泡壁速度空间受到显著限制,不同膨胀模式之间出现间隙;(4)对于QCD类理论,所有爆轰解(包括激波爆轰)都被排除;(5)这些效应强烈影响流体的动能分配,可能抑制最有效的配置,从而改变引力波信号预测。

Conclusion: 非共形动力学对一阶相变气泡膨胀具有关键影响,会产生新的流体动力学障碍机制,显著限制允许的气泡壁速度范围,并可能排除某些膨胀模式(如QCD类理论中的所有爆轰解)。这些效应对流体动能预算和引力波信号有重要影响,可能抑制最强的引力波产生配置。因此,准确建模相变及其引力波谱必须充分考虑非共形热力学效应,而不能简单依赖传统的共形或袋模型近似。

Abstract: We investigate the hydrodynamics of expanding bubbles in first-order phase transitions with non-conformal thermodynamics. We analyze a broad class of equations of state interpolating between bag-model descriptions, commonly used for electroweak transitions, and QCD-like theories. As a concrete benchmark, we determine the bubble solutions for pure SU(3) Yang-Mills theory. We uncover a new set of hydrodynamic obstructions to bubble expansion. These obstructions arise both at the bubble wall and along the fluid flow, and can partially or completely eliminate otherwise allowed solutions. We also identify a new class of solutions, which we dub "shocked detonations", consisting of ordinary detonations with an additional shock inserted in the rarefaction wave. As a consequence of these obstructions, the space of admissible bubble wall velocities is significantly constrained, with gaps appearing between different expansion regimes. For example, for QCD-like theories, all detonation solutions, including shocked detonations, are excluded. We show that these effects can strongly impact the kinetic energy budget of the fluid and, therefore, the resulting gravitational-wave signal, potentially suppressing the most efficient configurations. Our results highlight the importance of non-conformal dynamics for accurately modeling phase transitions and the resulting gravitational-wave spectrum. The code used to construct the bubble solutions is publicly available. For completeness, we also show how the interpolation between the bag-model and QCD-like limits can be realized holographically by varying the backreaction of matter fields on the geometry.

</details>


### [107] [A regulated zero-temperature construction of the Fundamental Modular Region in pure Yang--Mills theory and QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27896)
*Rodrigo Carmo Terin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将基本模区(FMR)表述为正则化复制权重Landau规范的零温极限,通过种群退火和集体basin跳跃在小型SU(2)基准测试中实现了计算验证,该构造无需参数化FMR边界即可定义绝对Landau规范,并可自然推广至完整的量子色动力学(QCD)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在格点规范理论中,Landau规范存在Gribov拷贝问题,即规范固定条件的多重解导致规范固定不唯一。传统方法难以准确定义和计算绝对Landau规范(对应于Faddeev-Popov算子最大本征值的规范固定)。本研究旨在通过零温极限的统计力学方法,在不显式参数化基本模区边界的情况下,构造一个可计算的绝对Landau规范定义框架。

Method: 采用正则化的复制权重Landau规范,在有限逆温度β下,测度局域化在绝对极小值上;主导修正项由Faddeev-Popov算子的逆主导;取β趋于无穷的零温极限得到基本模区的定义。在SU(2)规范理论的小型基准系统上,结合种群退火(population annealing)和集体basin跳跃(collective basin hopping)算法进行数值计算验证。该方法避免了显式参数化FMR边界,并提供了规范固定真空波泛函在FMR上的哈密顿量解释。

Result: 在小型SU(2)基准测试中成功实现了该构造的计算验证,证明了通过种群退火和集体basin跳跃方法可以有效地在有限温度下局域化到绝对极小值。数值结果支持零温极限下测度确实收敛到基本模区,且领头修正项由Faddeev-Popov算子的逆结构主导。该方法在计算上是可行的,为进一步推广到完整QCD理论提供了基础。

Conclusion: 本文成功将基本模区表述为正则化复制权重Landau规范的零温极限,提供了一种无需显式参数化FMR边界即可定义绝对Landau规范的新方法。该构造在SU(2)基准测试中得到计算验证,可自然推广至完整的量子色动力学理论,并具有清晰的哈密顿量解释。这为解决格点规范理论中的Gribov拷贝问题和精确计算规范依赖量提供了新的理论和计算框架。

Abstract: We formulate the Fundamental Modular Region (FMR) as the zero-temperature limit of regulated copy-weighted Landau gauges. At finite $β$, the measure localizes on absolute minima, and the leading correction is dominated by the inverse Faddeev--Popov (FP) operator. A small $SU(2)$ benchmark provides a computational realization through population annealing and collective basin hopping. Our construction defines absolute Landau gauge without parametrizing the FMR boundary and extends naturally to full quantum chromodynamics (QCD). It also admits a Hamiltonian interpretation in terms of the gauge-fixed vacuum wave functional on the FMR.

</details>


### [108] [Generalized Kazakov-Migdal Models on Graphs via Artin-Ihara $L$-function and Random Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27935)
*So Matsuura,Kazutoshi Ohta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Artin-Ihara L-函数在图上引入Kazakov-Migdal型规范理论,将循环图上的KM模型重构为由Schur测度控制的随机分割模型,在大Nc极限下精确求解并揭示了Gross-Witten-Wadia相变与Bose-Einstein凝聚的关系,同时建立了酉矩阵本征值密度与随机分割Maya图密度之间的确定联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作提出了多个KM型模型但缺乏统一描述框架。需要通过严格的数学工具(Artin-Ihara L-函数和调和分析)来统一理解这些模型,并在可精确求解的情况下揭示其深层物理机制,特别是相变现象的组合数学起源和物理意义。

Method: 采用Artin-Ihara L-函数在图上构建KM型规范理论的统一框架;利用群流形上的调和分析将循环图上的KM模型重构为由Schur测度支配的随机分割模型;在大Nc极限下对基本表示的KM模型进行精确求解;通过谱曲线推导液滴图像,建立酉矩阵本征值密度与Maya图密度的对应关系。

Result: 成功在大Nc极限下精确求解了循环图上基本表示的KM型模型;证明了Gross-Witten-Wadia相变精确发生在Young图的极限形状触及允许表示空间边界时;阐明了该相变与Bose-Einstein凝聚的密切联系;揭示了强/弱耦合对偶性具有自然的组合解释,表现为Young图与其补图的交换,这反映了Artin-Ihara L-函数的函数方程;从谱曲线导出了液滴图像,建立了酉矩阵本征值密度与随机分割Maya图密度之间的确定关系。

Conclusion: 本文通过Artin-Ihara L-函数成功构建了图上KM型规范理论的统一框架,并利用随机分割模型的语言在大Nc极限下精确求解了循环图情形。研究揭示了Gross-Witten-Wadia相变的几何起源(Young图触及边界)、其与Bose-Einstein凝聚的物理联系,以及强/弱耦合对偶性的组合数学本质。通过谱曲线建立的液滴图像为理解酉矩阵本征值与随机分割之间的深层对应提供了清晰的几何描述,为进一步研究规范理论与可积系统、组合数学的交叉提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce Kazakov-Migdal (KM)-type gauge theories on graphs via the Artin-Ihara $L$-function, providing a unified description of the models proposed in prior works. Using harmonic analysis on the group manifold, we reformulate the KM-type model on the cycle graph as a random partition model governed by the Schur measure. We exactly solve the KM-type model on the cycle graph in the fundamental representation as the random partition model in the large $N_c$ limit and demonstrate that the Gross-Witten-Wadia phase transition occurs precisely when the limiting shape of the Young diagram touches the boundary of the allowed representation space. We further clarify that this phase transition is intimately related to Bose-Einstein condensation, and the strong/weak coupling duality possesses a natural combinatorial interpretation as the exchange between the Young diagram and its complement, reflecting the functional equation of the Artin-Ihara $L$-function. We also establish a definitive relationship between the eigenvalue density of the unitary matrix and the Maya diagram density of the random partitions by deriving a droplet picture from the spectral curve.

</details>


### [109] [Mega-Space Current Algebra and Green-Schwarz Geometry in Heterotic String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27988)
*Machiko Hatsuda,Shin Sasaki,Masaya Yata*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在显式T对偶框架下研究了杂化弦理论中的洛伦兹联络和规范联络,通过构造包含洛伦兹和规范部门的超空间流代数,几何地嵌入了杂化Chern-Simons结构,并证明了Green-Schwarz反常消除条件源于弦协变导数代数的Jacobi恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索杂化弦理论α'修正中含挠率的洛伦兹联络与Yang-Mills联络之间的Bergshoeff-de Roo(BdR)型平行关系。为了在显式T对偶的框架下统一处理这两种联络结构,需要发展一个能够同时容纳洛伦兹部门和规范部门的几何框架,从而揭示杂化弦理论中的深层对称性和结构。

Method: 采用世界面流代数方法,在一个同时包含洛伦兹部门和规范部门的"超空间"(mega-space)中进行研究。通过推广Poláček-Siegel方案,计算扩展生成元的对易子,从而确定广义联络。利用弦协变导数的代数结构(称为"nachos"算符∇_M)及其Jacobi恒等式来分析理论的几何性质和一致性条件。

Result: 成功证明了超空间流代数能够将杂化Chern-Simons结构几何地嵌入到广义标架场(generalized vielbeins)和曲率中。揭示了Green-Schwarz反常消除条件可以从弦协变导数代数的Jacobi恒等式自然导出,这为理解杂化弦理论的反常消除机制提供了新的代数-几何视角。

Conclusion: 本文在显式T对偶框架下建立了统一处理杂化弦理论中洛伦兹联络和规范联络的超空间流代数形式体系。该框架不仅揭示了BdR型平行关系的几何本质,将杂化Chern-Simons结构自然嵌入广义几何量中,而且证明了Green-Schwarz反常消除这一关键物理条件源于代数结构的Jacobi恒等式。这为理解杂化弦理论的α'修正和T对偶对称性提供了深刻的代数-几何统一图景。

Abstract: We study the Lorentz and gauge connections in heterotic string theories within a framework of manifest T-duality. A central motivation is the Bergshoeff--de Roo (BdR) type parallelism between the torsionful Lorentz and Yang-Mills connections in heterotic $α'$-corrections. We formulate worldsheet current algebras in a mega-space that incorporates the Lorentz and gauge sectors simultaneously. By generalizing the Poláček-Siegel scheme, we evaluate the commutators of the extended generators and determine the generalized connections. We show that this mega-space current algebra geometrically embeds the heterotic Chern--Simons structures into the generalized vielbeins and curvatures. We also show that the Green-Schwarz anomaly cancellation condition follows from the Jacobi identity for the algebra of stringy covariant derivatives, the ``nachos'' operators $\triangleright_{\mathcal{M}}$.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum Trigonometric Spin Ruijsenaars-Schneider Models from $K$-theoretic Coulomb Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28043)
*Gleb Arutyunov,Lukas Hardi,Rob Klabbers*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用K-理论Coulomb分支代数框架对三角自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型进行量子化,通过L-算符代数构造可积自旋链,并导出量子化物理自旋变量的对易关系和运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的三角自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型已有成熟的描述,但其量子化版本需要系统性的数学框架。最近发展的K-理论Coulomb分支理论为4d N=2规范理论提供了新工具,为该模型的量子化提供了代数几何基础,使得可以利用单极子算符和量子群结构来系统地处理具有自旋自由度的N粒子可积系统的量子化问题。

Method: 采用K-理论Coulomb分支代数框架,针对秩为N的ℓ节点项链型箭图(necklace quiver)对应的4d N=2规范理论进行研究。核心代数工具是从阿贝尔化的微小荷单极子算符导出的L-算符代数,该代数将项链箭图转化为可积自旋链。通过构造对易哈密顿量族来实现量子化,并证明最低阶哈密顿量与K-理论Coulomb分支代数中水平量子loop代数的量子行列式的第一模式相一致,其Bethe子代数生成极大对易哈密顿量族。

Result: 成功构造了三角自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型的量子化版本。证明了最低哈密顿量与量子行列式第一模式的等价性,建立了完整的对易哈密顿量族。导出了量子化物理自旋变量的对易关系和量子运动方程,为该可积系统提供了完整的量子代数描述框架。

Conclusion: 本文通过K-理论Coulomb分支代数方法成功实现了N粒子ℓ自旋态三角自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型的量子化。L-算符代数及其产生的可积结构提供了系统的量子化框架,Bethe子代数生成的对易哈密顿量族确保了系统的可积性,量子自旋变量的对易关系和运动方程的导出完善了该模型的量子理论。该工作展示了现代规范理论工具在可积系统量子化中的有效应用。

Abstract: We quantize the trigonometric spin Ruijsenaars-Schneider model of $N$ particles each with $\ell$ spin states using the recently developed description of the classical model in terms of the $K$-theoretic Coulomb branch of the 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ quiver gauge theory for the necklace quiver with $\ell$ nodes of rank $N$. The main algebraic tool is an algebra of $L$-operators derived from abelianized monopole operators of minuscule charge, which turns the necklace quiver into an integrable spin chain by producing a family of commuting Hamiltonians. We show that the lowest Hamiltonian coincides with the first mode of the quantum determinant of the horizontal quantum loop algebra living inside the $K$-theoretic Coulomb branch algebra, whose Bethe subalgebra generates a maximal family of commuting Hamiltonians. Finally, we derive the commutation relations and quantum equations of motion of the quantized physical spin variables.

</details>


### [111] [Interior zeros of supersymmetric indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28143)
*Yu Nakayama,Tadashi Okazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究超对称指标的内部零点问题，发现内部零点的存在与否可通过算术系数的指数增长行为从q-级数展开中直接检测，并证明内部零点会阻碍自由或s-禁闭的红外描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超对称指标在理解量子场论的红外行为中起关键作用，但其内部零点（|q|<1区域的零点）的存在性及物理意义尚不清楚。研究者希望建立内部零点与底层算术结构的联系，并探索其对红外物理的影响，特别是能否从有限的q-级数展开中检测这些零点。

Method: 研究采用以下方法：1) 将超对称指标与超对称zeta函数的算术系数δ(ν)关联，证明内部零点存在当且仅当δ(ν)以指数速率增长，增长率由最近的零点位置决定；2) 利用每个δ(ν)可由有限多个q-级数系数确定的性质，从展开式中检测内部零点；3) 以4维N=1 SU(2) SQCD为例验证内部零点阻碍自由或s-禁闭红外描述；4) 采用巨引力子展开方法研究有限N效应，通过单个巨引力子能量定位内部零点，并在N=4 U(N) Schur指标中验证。

Result: 研究获得以下结果：1) 建立了内部零点存在性与算术系数δ(ν)指数增长的等价关系；2) 证明内部零点可从q-级数的有限项展开中检测；3) 在4维N=1 SU(2) SQCD理论中明确展示了内部零点如何阻碍自由或s-禁闭的红外描述；4) 在巨引力子展开框架下，确认内部零点是有限N效应，其位置由单个巨引力子的能量决定；5) 在N=4 U(N) Schur指标中成功验证了上述理论预言。

Conclusion: 本文建立了超对称指标内部零点的完整理论框架：内部零点与算术系数的指数增长率直接相关，可从有限q-级数展开中检测，并对红外物理具有重要约束作用——阻碍特定类型的红外描述。在有限N理论中，内部零点是可通过巨引力子能量精确定位的有限N效应。这为理解超对称理论的非微扰性质和红外动力学提供了新的诊断工具和理论洞察。

Abstract: A supersymmetric index has interior zeros ($|q|<1$) if and only if the arithmetic coefficients $δ(ν)$ underlying the supersymmetric zeta function grow exponentially at a rate set by the nearest zero. Each $δ(ν)$ follows from finitely many $q$-series coefficients, so interior zeros are detectable from the expansion alone and obstruct a free or s-confining infrared description, as we show for 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ $SU(2)$ SQCD. With a giant graviton expansion interior zeros are a finite $N$ effect, located by the energy of one giant graviton, verified for the $\mathcal{N}=4$ $U(N)$ Schur index.

</details>


### [112] [Nonlocal four-fermion theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28160)
*F. M. Belchior,J. R. Nascimento,A. Yu. Petrov,P. J. Porfirio*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种非局域四费米子理论,其中狄拉克算符通过整函数型非局域形状因子变形。通过引入辅助标量场和平均场近似,导出了动力学质量的能隙方程,并分析了两种代表性形状因子(双曲型和振荡型)对临界耦合的相反影响,同时将理论推广至有限温度和有限密度情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局域四费米子理论(如NJL/Gross-Neveu模型)在处理紫外发散时存在困难。本文旨在通过引入非局域形状因子来改进狄拉克算符,系统研究非局域性如何影响手征对称性自发破缺和动力学质量生成机制,特别是探讨不同类型的非局域形状因子对临界耦合常数的调控作用。

Method: 采用平均场近似方法,通过引入辅助标量场导出有效作用量和能隙方程。关键技术是将非局域形状因子视为狄拉克算符的矩阵函数,在由单位算符和动量斜杠算符生成的闭代数中处理逆传播子。采用IR/UV匹配方法,在中间能标处分割动量积分,分别在红外和紫外区域进行解析展开,并与局域NJL/Gross-Neveu结果对比。对两种形状因子$f_{I}(\not{\!\partial})=e^{-\not{\partial}/Λ}$(双曲型)和$f_{II}(\not{\!\partial})=e^{-i\not{\partial}/Λ}$(振荡型)进行具体计算。通过Matsubara求和和修正的围道积分表示推广至有限温度和有限密度情形。

Result: 获得了两种形状因子的能隙核显式表达式。双曲型形状因子增强能隙积分并降低临界耦合,而振荡型形状因子抑制能隙积分并提高临界耦合。在$Λ\to\infty$极限下成功恢复了局域理论的热能隙方程。研究表明非局域形状因子的选择对手征相变的临界行为具有本质性影响。

Conclusion: 本文建立了完整的非局域四费米子理论框架,证明了非局域形状因子作为矩阵函数的正确处理方式是理论自洽性的关键。双曲型和振荡型形状因子对临界耦合产生相反的调控效果,为通过非局域性控制手征对称性破缺提供了理论依据。有限温度和有限密度的推广表明该理论框架可用于研究极端条件下的强相互作用物质性质,并在高能标截断趋于无穷时平滑过渡到标准局域理论。

Abstract: In this work, we formulate and analyze a nonlocal four-fermion theory in which the usual Dirac operator is deformed by an entire nonlocal form factor. After introducing an auxiliary scalar field, we derive the mean-field effective action and the corresponding gap equation for the dynamical mass. A central technical point of the analysis is that the nonlocal form factor is a matrix function of the Dirac operator, so the inverse propagator must be treated as an element of the closed algebra generated by the identity and $\not{\!p}$ operators, rather than as a purely scalar quantity. We obtain explicit expressions for the gap kernel for two representative choices of a form factor, $f_{I}(\not{\!\partial})=e^{-\not{\partial}/Λ}$ and $f_{II}(\not{\!\partial})=e^{-i\not{\partial}/Λ}$. Following the IR/UV matching method used in the recent Dirac-like nonlocal spinor theory [1], the momentum integral is split at an intermediate scale $M\ll Ω\ll Λ$, expanded analytically in the infrared and ultraviolet regions, and compared with the usual local NJL/Gross-Neveu result. We show that the hyperbolic form factor enhances the gap integral and lowers the critical coupling, whereas the oscillatory form factor suppresses it and raises the critical coupling. The finite-temperature and finite-density extension is formulated through Matsubara sums and a corrected contour representation, with the local thermal gap equation recovered in the limit $Λ\to\infty$.

</details>


### [113] [Light-Front approach to $4d$ massless Higher-Spin interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28183)
*Mattia Serrani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了4维无质量高自旋场相互作用理论，通过光前方法分析四阶庞加莱代数的闭合性，系统分类了局域高自旋理论并揭示了其与天球共形场论、树图振幅及规范代数的深层联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高自旋场相互作用理论面临一致性问题，特别是在四阶相互作用层面如何满足庞加莱对称性约束。需要系统研究光锥全纯约束和非全纯约束，分类可能的局域高自旋理论，并阐明其与天球共形场论OPE结合律、树图振幅消失条件以及运动学代数Jacobi恒等式之间的内在关系。

Method: 采用光前(Light-Front)方法分析四阶庞加莱代数闭合性。首先求解平直时空中的光锥四阶全纯约束，分类一阶和二阶导数理论(对应高自旋推广的规范和引力相互作用)。然后研究全纯约束与天球CFT的OPE结合律、树图振幅、规范代数Jacobi恒等式的等价关系。最后分析包含MHV和反MHV顶点的非全纯四阶约束，引入高阶导数三次顶点后，利用旋量螺旋度形式和一致因子化的局域性条件分类所有幺正局域高自旋理论。

Result: 证明了存在无穷多个包含有限或无限多场的相互作用局域高自旋理论。成功分类了所有一阶和二阶导数理论,这些是自对偶Yang-Mills理论和引力理论的高自旋推广的一致子扇区，也是手征高自旋引力的截断。恢复了Yang-Mills理论和引力理论的存在性，证实了相互作用多引力子理论的不一致性。发现引入高阶导数三次顶点后存在完整四阶约束的非平凡解，识别出新的局域准手征理论族，并确定了所有局域高自旋四点振幅。

Conclusion: 通过光前方法成功建立了4维无质量高自旋相互作用理论的系统分类框架。揭示了光锥全纯约束与天球CFT的OPE结合律、树图振幅消失条件及规范代数Jacobi恒等式的等价性。证明了包含高阶导数项的局域高自旋理论的存在性和一致性，为构造完整的高自旋相互作用理论提供了新的途径，并通过旋量螺旋度形式和一致因子化方法完整确定了局域高自旋四点振幅。

Abstract: This thesis studies $4d$ massless higher-spin interactions in the Light-Front approach by analysing the closure of the Poincaré algebra at quartic order. We first solve the light-cone quartic holomorphic constraint in flat space and show the existence of infinitely many interacting local higher-spin theories with either a finite or infinite number of fields. We classify all one- and two-derivative theories, corresponding to higher-spin extensions of gauge and gravitational interactions. These are consistent subsectors of higher-spin extensions of self-dual Yang--Mills and gravity, themselves truncations of Chiral Higher-Spin Gravity. We then clarify the relation between the OPE associativity in celestial CFT, the vanishing of tree-level amplitudes for generic kinematics, the Jacobi identity of the associated ''gauge algebra'' (kinematical algebra), and the light-cone holomorphic constraints. Finally, we investigate the non-holomorphic quartic constraint involving both MHV and anti-MHV vertices. We recover the existence of Yang-Mills theory and gravity, and the inconsistency of interacting multi-graviton theories. We then show that once higher-derivative cubic vertices are included, nontrivial solutions to the full quartic constraint exist. We classify all unitary local higher-spin theories, identify new families of local quasi-chiral theories, and determine all local higher-spin four-point amplitudes using the spinor-helicity formalism together with locality in the form of consistent factorisation.

</details>


### [114] [Missing Descendants in the Carrollian Conformal Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28400)
*Yu-fan Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了完整的Carrollian共形表示，包含由特殊共形生成元K^0连续降低到初级算符的独立下降链及其平移后代，推导了相应的局域算符、轨道结构和Casimir算符，并通过全局两点Ward恒等式确定了关联函数的运动学因子和选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Carrollian共形代数中，由于对易关系[P^0,K^0]=0的存在，标准的由平移后代定义的共形族无法包含K^0连续作用于初级算符所产生的独立下降链。这导致需要构建完整的Carrollian共形表示理论，以全面描述该对称性及其在平坦时空全息中的应用。

Method: 通过在标准共形族基础上引入K^0连续降低初级算符所生成的独立下降链及其所有平移后代，构建完整的Carrollian共形表示。推导相应的局域算符形式和轨道结构，在2维和3维情况下验证Casimir算符。利用全局两点Ward恒等式确定不同轨道构型下磁性非接触分支和电性接触分支的运动学因子和选择规则。

Result: 成功构建了完整的Carrollian共形表示，包含所有独立的算符下降链。在2维和3维中验证了Casimir算符的正确性。发现与通常共形对称性不同，Carrollian共形对称性一般只能将关联函数确定到Carrollian不变量的任意函数，而非常数。完整表示包含满足𝒞₂=m²=κρ-β²>0(2维)和𝒞₂=m²=κρ-β⃗²>0(3维)的扇区，可能描述平坦全息中的有质量态。

Conclusion: 本文完整构建了Carrollian共形表示理论，揭示了其与通常共形对称性的本质差异：关联函数仅被确定到Carrollian不变量的任意函数。理论中存在正Casimir扇区(𝒞₂>0)，为平坦时空全息中描述有质量态提供了理论框架，对理解平坦全息的动力学结构具有重要意义。

Abstract: In the Carrollian conformal algebra, the relation $[P^0,K^0]=0$ implies that $K^0$ annihilates all operators generated from a primary by temporal translation $P^0$. The standard conformal family defined by translation descendants does not contain the independent descendant chain that $K^0$ lowers successively to the primary. We construct the complete Carrollian conformal representation by including this chain together with all of its translation descendants. We derived the corresponding local operators, orbit structures, and checked their Casimirs in 2D and 3D. For each orbit configuration, the global two-point Ward identities fix the kinematic factors and selection rules for both magnetic non-contact branches and electric contact branches. Unlike ordinary conformal symmetry, Carrollian conformal symmetry generally determines the correlators only up to arbitrary functions of Carrollian invariants rather than constants. The complete representation contains sectors with $\mathcal{C}_2=m^2=κρ-β^2>0$ for 2D and $\mathcal{C}_2=m^2=κρ-\vecβ^{\,2}>0$ for 3D, which may describe massive states in flat holography.

</details>


### [115] [Covariant variation and its applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28420)
*Wen-Bin Liu,Jiang Long*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文定义了张量场的协变变分算子，通过结合李导数与度规变分构造，该算子保持度规、缩并和Hodge对偶性，但其对易子因反常而不封闭。文章推导了其代数和几何性质，并应用于超旋转沿零超曲面的反常分析及任意维时空中的张量场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的李导数在处理张量场变分时无法同时保持度规不变性和几何结构的协变性。需要构造一种新的变分算子，既能保持重要的几何性质（度规、缩并、Hodge对偶），又能统一处理时空结构和洛伦兹结构的场，并揭示其在渐近对称性（如超旋转）中的物理意义。

Method: 通过将李导数与度规变分相结合，定义协变变分算子；系统推导该算子的代数性质（保度规性、保缩并性、保Hodge对偶性）和几何性质；分析其对易子的非封闭性及反常项；将协变变分与Kosmann导数结合，构造具有时空和洛伦兹双重结构场的总协变变分；引入包含仿射联络、李导数和协变变分的扩展算子族；应用于四维零超曲面上的超旋转反常和任意维时空中的p-形式场（特别是d=2p+2维情形）。

Result: 成功构造了保持度规、缩并和Hodge对偶的协变变分算子；证明其对易子存在反常项导致不封闭；建立了与Kosmann导数的关系，并构造了度规李导数框架下的总协变变分；发现在四维时空中，超旋转方向的协变变分反常会在零超曲面产生电磁螺旋度流；对任意维时空的张量场（特别是d=2p+2维的p-形式）给出了系统的协变变分理论及其反常分析。

Conclusion: 协变变分算子为张量场的变分理论提供了新的几何工具，它保持关键几何性质但具有反常结构。该算子与Kosmann导数的结合完善了度规李导数的理论框架，其反常项具有深刻的物理意义——在渐近对称性分析中揭示了电磁螺旋度流等守恒量。该理论框架可推广至任意维时空，为微分几何和理论物理（特别是引力和规范场理论）提供了统一的数学描述。

Abstract: We define a covariant variation of tensor fields by combining its Lie derivative with the metric variation. This operator preserves the metric, contractions, and Hodge duality, but its commutator is not closed due to an anomaly. We derive its algebraic and geometric properties, and compare it with the Kosmann derivative. Combining the covariant variation with Kosmann derivative gives total covariant variation for the fields with both spacetime and Lorentz structure, all of which belong to the metric Lie derivative. Moreover, we introduce families of extended operators which contain the affine connection, Lie derivative, and covariant variation. From the anomaly of the covariant variation along the superrotation, an electromagnetic helicity flux appears at null hypersurfaces in four dimensions. We also apply the covariant variation and its anomaly to tensor fields in arbitrary spacetime dimensions, and especially focus on the $p$-forms in $d=2p+2$ dimensions.

</details>


### [116] [Hayden--Preskill recovery at finite temperature on a quantum processor: dynamics and initial state from the SYK model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28486)
*Jeongho Bang,Moongul Byun,Kyoungho Cho,Keun-Young Kim,Hyeonsoo Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Hayden-Preskill信息恢复协议,引入SWAP门使扰频器与初始态相关,并考虑有限温度效应。在IBM超导量子处理器上使用8个Majorana费米子的稀疏SYK哈密顿量验证了该协议,表明强扰频对成功恢复信息至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的Hayden-Preskill恢复协议中,注入后的扰频器与初始态不相关。本研究旨在扩展该协议,探索在更真实的物理条件下(扰频器与初始态相关、有限温度环境)的量子信息恢复机制,并在实际量子硬件上验证理论预测。

Method: 采用两种扩展方式:(1)使用SWAP门建立扰频器与初始态的关联;(2)引入有限温度条件。通过后选择概率和条件保真度评估信息恢复成功率。在均匀算符扩散假设下推导晚时解析估计,并与数值结果对比。在IBM超导量子处理器上实现该协议,使用N=8个Majorana费米子的二元稀疏SYK哈密顿量进行实验验证,采用基于SWAP的误差缓解方案优化结果。

Result: 理论分析表明信息可以成功恢复,后选择概率非零且条件保真度较高。两者均与温度成正比,反映了低温下初始态纠缠的减少。均匀算符扩散假设下的晚时解析估计与数值结果吻合良好,证实了强扰频对成功恢复信息的重要性。IBM量子处理器的实验数据保留了定性的恢复动力学特征,基于SWAP的误差缓解方案显著提升了后选择概率和条件保真度。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将Hayden-Preskill协议推广到扰频器与初始态相关及有限温度的更真实场景,理论和实验均验证了量子信息的成功恢复。温度依赖性揭示了热纠缠对信息恢复的影响,强扰频是恢复成功的关键机制。在实际量子硬件上的实现证明了该协议的可行性,误差缓解技术有效改善了实验性能,为量子信息恢复和黑洞信息悖论研究提供了新的实验路径。

Abstract: In the original Hayden--Preskill recovery, the post-injection scrambler and initial state are {\it not related}. We extend this setup in two ways: by using a SWAP gate so that the scrambler and initial state are {\it related}, and by considering recovery at {\it finite} temperature. For this modified protocol, we show that the information is successfully recovered in the sense that the postselection probability is non-negligible and the conditional fidelity is large. We find that both the postselection probability and the conditional fidelity are proportional to temperature, reflecting the reduced entanglement of the initial state at lower temperatures. We also derive their late-time analytic estimates under the assumption of uniform operator spreading and show that they agree well with the numerical results. This demonstrates that strong scrambling is important for successful information recovery. Implementing the protocol on an IBM superconducting processor using a binary sparse SYK Hamiltonian with $N = 8$ Majoranas, we observe that the data retain the qualitative recovery dynamics and that a SWAP-based error-mitigation scheme improves both the postselection probability and the conditional fidelity.

</details>


### [117] [Soft charges and zero modes at null boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28543)
*Dušan Đorđ ević,Olivera Miskovic,Antonia Montecinos,Tatjana Vukašinac*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究零边界(null boundaries)的渐近对称性,揭示了全局零模如何修改场论的正则结构,并导致新的准局域边界可观测量及其对荷代数的影响。该现象适用于零无穷远和有限距离(如黑洞视界)的各类零边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零边界的渐近对称性为理解场论软扇区提供了重要途径,可揭示守恒荷和无穷维对称代数。虽然这些结构通常与剩余规范变换相关,但零边界还表现出特征性的全局零模不确定性,其物理后果尚未被充分探索。本文旨在研究这一零模如何影响零边界场论的正则结构及其物理意义。

Method: 本文采用理论分析方法,系统研究零边界处场论的正则结构。重点分析全局零模如何修改标准的正则形式体系,探讨其对边界可观测量和对称性代数的影响。研究涵盖零无穷远和有限距离零边界(如黑洞视界)等不同情形。

Result: 研究表明,全局零模显著修改了零边界场论的正则结构,导致了一个新的准局域边界可观测量(quasilocal edge observable)的出现。这一新可观测量对荷代数(charge algebra)产生了重要影响。研究证实该现象具有普遍性,可以出现在各种类型的零边界上,包括零无穷远和有限距离的零边界(如黑洞视界)。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了零边界全局零模对场论正则结构的根本性影响,发现了新的准局域边界可观测量及其对荷代数的作用。这一发现深化了对零边界渐近对称性和软扇区物理的理解,为研究从零无穷远到黑洞视界等各类零边界提供了统一的理论框架,拓展了对场论边界动力学的认识。

Abstract: Asymptotic symmetries at null boundaries provide a powerful window into the soft sector of field theories, revealing conserved charges and infinite-dimensional symmetry algebras beyond the standard bulk dynamics. While these structures are usually associated with residual gauge transformations, null boundaries also exhibit a characteristic global zero-mode ambiguity, whose physical consequences are less explored. We discuss how this zero mode modifies the canonical structure of field theories at null boundaries. In particular, we show that it gives rise to a new quasilocal edge observable, with consequences to the charge algebra. It can arise on any kind of null boundaries, both at the null infinity and the finite distance such as a black hole horizon.

</details>


### [118] [Learning to Trace Seiberg Dualities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28628)
*Jonathan J. Heckman,Shani Meynet,Alessandro Mininno,Gary Shiu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文使用机器学习方法研究超对称箭图规范理论的Seiberg对偶性问题,通过transformer和多层感知器等神经网络架构来学习建立箭图变异,在中等规模箭图(约10个节点)上超越了确定性算法,并结合路径搜索算法进一步提升了效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶性在物理系统中具有重要作用,但即使理论框架明确,实际判断两个系统是否对偶在计算上仍然具有挑战性。本文旨在解决如何高效判定超对称箭图规范理论的Seiberg对偶性这一问题,这在数学上涉及箭图变异的建立,是"学习解结"主题的变体。研究动机在于开发实用工具以评估不同对偶性的计算复杂度,并探索不同网络架构如何学习追踪Seiberg对偶性。

Method: 采用机器学习方法处理Seiberg对偶性问题,主要使用transformer和多层感知器(MLP)等不同神经网络架构来学习箭图变异。研究针对具有约10个节点的中等规模箭图进行实验,并将神经网络与成熟的路径搜索算法(类似"箭图的谷歌地图")相结合,形成混合搜索策略。通过比较不同网络架构与确定性算法的性能,评估机器学习方法在建立对偶性方面的效率和准确性。

Result: 对于具有约10个节点的中等规模箭图,transformer和多层感知器等神经网络架构的性能优于传统的确定性算法。当神经网络与路径搜索算法结合使用时,搜索策略的效率和准确性得到了进一步提升。研究成功展示了机器学习方法在处理Seiberg对偶性问题上的实用性,并揭示了不同网络架构学习追踪对偶性的方式。

Conclusion: 本文证明了机器学习方法,特别是transformer和多层感知器架构,能够有效处理超对称箭图规范理论的Seiberg对偶性问题,在中等规模箭图上超越确定性算法。结合路径搜索算法的混合策略进一步增强了性能。这项工作不仅提供了评估对偶性计算复杂度的实用工具,也为理解神经网络如何学习理论物理问题提供了洞见。研究者认为此类问题可以作为前沿AI模型应用于理论物理的有用基准测试。

Abstract: Dualities play an important role in establishing both microscopic and emergent phenomena in a wide range of physical systems. In practice, though, it can often be computationally challenging to establish when two systems are dual, even when all of the "rules of the game" are well-known. Said differently, when confronted with two systems, how can one efficiently establish that they are in fact dual? In this paper we use machine learning methods to address this question for Seiberg dualities of supersymmetric quiver gauge theories. Mathematically, this involves establishing mutations of quivers, which is in turn a variation on the theme of "learning to unknot". On the one hand, this leads us to a practical tool for establishing the computational complexity of different dualities. On the other hand, it also allows us to study how different network architectures learn how to trace Seiberg dualities. We find that for quivers with a modest number of quiver nodes (of order $10$), different network architectures consisting of transformers and multi-layer perceptrons tend to outperform deterministic algorithms. Supplementing the network by well-established pathfinder algorithms (essentially "Google Maps for quivers") leads to an additional improvement in the efficiency and accuracy of the search strategy. We anticipate that this class of questions can serve as a useful benchmark for frontier AI models applied to theoretical physics.

</details>
